| Haruka | Y-Yes... I dropped something important. | talk |
| Saki | Oh dear! Come on, we have to find it quickly! | talk |
| Ichika | What did you drop? | talk |
| Haruka | You're going to help me look? | talk |
| Saki | I mean, of course! | talk |
| Haruka | Thank you...<br>I'm looking for a black and white penguin keychain, wearing a red scarf. | talk |
| Saki | Penguin? Oh, didn't we find that just now? | talk |
| Ichika | Yes. Kiritani, are you talking about this penguin?<br>We found it on the floor just now. | talk |
| Haruka | Ah! Thank goodness! | talk |
| Haruka | Thank you so much for finding it for me. | talk |
| Saki | Hehe♪ You're most welcome! | talk |
| Haruka | Please allow me to do something to repay you for this. | talk |
| Ichika | Huh? There's no need.<br>We just happened to see it, that's all. | talk |
| Haruka | But if you hadn't... | talk |
| Ichika | I get it.<br>This is something really important to you, right? | talk |
| Haruka | Yes. I have a lot of other penguin merchandise too, but this is the one I've had for the longest time. | talk |
| Saki | You like penguins, Haruka? | talk |
| Haruka | I just think they look so cute with their round bodies. And I can't help but keep buying the penguin merchandise I see at the aquarium. | talk |
| Saki | Even idol celebrities like Haruka visit aquariums too? | talk |
| Haruka | Is that...strange? | talk |
| Saki | No way, it's not strange at all!<br>I'm glad to hear that, actually. | talk |
| Haruka | Why? | talk |
| Ichika | Well, it helps us see that you're a high school girl like us too. | talk |
| Saki | Actually, I've been wondering how I should talk to you...<br>But now I know you're just a normal classmate! | talk |
| Haruka | Oh... | talk |
| Saki | Hey, I'd really love to get to know you better!<br>Would you like to have lunch together later? | talk |
| Ichika | How about lunch together to repay us, then? | talk |
| Haruka | If that's what you want, then I'm happy to oblige. | talk |
| Haruka | And...thank you for not seeing me as an idol... | talk |
| Saki | Huh? Did you just say something? | talk |
| Haruka | No, nothing at all. I'm looking forward to lunchtime. | talk |

| Haruka | The lunch stand was really crowded. | talk |
| Saki | We were there during peak period, after all.<br>But I managed to get what I wanted! | talk |
| Ichika | Me too. What about you, Kiritani? | talk |
| Haruka | Yes. I bought what I wanted too. | talk |
| Saki | So we all got what we wanted, then!<br>What did you buy, Ichi? | talk |

| Haruka | That doesn't sound very nutritionally balanced...<br>Do you also eat foods like salad? | talk |

| Saki | Th-That's not true!<br>I got the pasta as my main meal... | talk |
| Haruka | The school store had a lot of snacks for sale. | talk |
| Saki | Yeah!<br>And this one here's the latest release! | talk |
| Ichika | You're always only buying snacks... | talk |
| Saki | Says the girl who only eats yakisoba buns. | talk |
| Haruka | Hehehe. | talk |
| Haruka | (They're childhood friends, right? They get along so well.) | talk |
| Saki | Ah, by the way, what did you buy, Haruka? | talk |
| Haruka | Oh, I bought... | talk |
| Ichika | Salad and... | talk |
| Saki | Chicken breast meat? | talk |
| Haruka | These items were the most nutritionally balanced of all the food being sold in the store. Am I being odd? | talk |
| Saki | No. It's amazing you even take that into consideration! | talk |
| Ichika | Completely different from us since we only eat what we like. | talk |
| Saki | I think I've seen you mention on a TV interview or something that you follow a pretty restrictive diet, right? | talk |
| Haruka | Yes, I do. | talk |
| Haruka | I'm not an idol anymore, so I can quit if I like, but... | talk |
| Ichika | But you keep doing it anyway? | talk |
| Haruka | One time, I did try to stop both my diet and training, but it made me feel so restless. | talk |
| Haruka | So in the end, I'm still doing my routine from before. I just don't know when to give up, huh? | talk |
| Saki | Haruka... | talk |
| Ichika | Hm... | talk |
| Ichika | I mean, why not? | talk |
| Haruka | Huh? | talk |
| Ichika | If carrying out your routine makes you feel calmer, that means it's something essential to you. | talk |
| Haruka | Essential... | talk |
| Saki | I agree!<br>Just like how snacks are essential to me! | talk |
| Ichika | That's completely different, Saki. | talk |
| Saki | Ichi, you're so mean to me... | talk |
| Haruka | Hehehe. A little bit of snacking is fine.<br>The more you eat, though, the more you should try to exercise. | talk |
| Saki | Oh, I exercise lots in my school club, so I'm safe! | talk |
| Ichika | Maybe I should try some salad once in a while... | talk |
| Saki | Hey, that's not fair!<br>Okay, I'll switch out my pasta for salad tomorrow too! | talk |
| Ichika | Shouldn't you be switching your snacks for salad instead? | talk |
| Haruka | Hehe... Haha! | talk |
| Ichika | See? She's laughing at you. | talk |
| Saki | Ugh... | talk |
| Haruka | Hehehe, I wasn't laughing at you. | talk |
| Haruka | Thank you, both of you. | talk |

| Haruka | Don't worry, it's nothing. Come on, let's eat. | talk |

| Minori | What is it? | talk |
| Haruka | Something smells tasty. | talk |
| Minori | Oh, you're right!<br>Smells like butter to me? | talk |
| Haruka | You've got a good nose. | talk |
| Haruka | Look, that's a cream puff store over there. | talk |
| Minori | Huh. I didn't know there were any food stores that way. | talk |
| Minori | What a lucky find! Let's get something from there. Their cream puffs look so yummy. They don't skimp on the cream! | talk |
| Haruka | They do look tasty... | talk |
| Haruka | You know what, I'll just wait here while you go and get something for yourself. | talk |
| Minori | Why aren't you coming, Haruka? Oh, I'm so slow. | talk |
| Minori | You're on a diet?<br>You mentioned it in a magazine interview some time ago, right? | talk |
| Haruka | That's right, I watch my calories, and those puffs really are loaded with cream... | talk |
| Minori | Oh no! Don't tell me cream puffs are calorie bombs! | talk |
| Haruka | They're not. Compared to other desserts, they're not terribly calorific. | talk |
| Haruka | Of course, if you eat a whole lot of them, that's a different story. | talk |
| Haruka | The best time for a sweet treat like this is after lunch. | talk |
| Haruka | When your metabolism is up and running, fill up on vegetables and some protein. | talk |
| Haruka | Then they won't cause you a sudden spike in blood sugar. | talk |
| Minori | You're so careful about what and when you're eating. | talk |
| Minori | But aren't you really a sucker for sweets? I saw you stop in front of that cake shop earlier, looking longingly at the display. | talk |
| Haruka | Um... Yeah, you got me. | talk |
| Minori | Then why don't you have them once in a while? | talk |
| Minori | I think that when you get your energy from something you love, it powers you up more, more! | talk |
| Haruka | Oh... | talk |
| Haruka | (She might have a point.) | talk |
| Haruka | (Training hard and sticking to a healthy diet contribute to my growth as an idol...) | talk |
| Haruka | (But doing things that make me happy is also important for staying motivated.) | talk |
| Haruka | You always surprise me with your insight, Minori. | talk |
| Haruka | Let's go get some cream puffs. | talk |
| Minori | Yay! Happy cream puff time with Haruka! | talk |
| Haruka | Oh, you can choose from six different types of cream filling. | talk |
| Minori | Six types, wow!<br>Hmm, I can't choose... | talk |
| Minori | Which one are you getting, Haru— | talk |

| Haruka | So this is where we'll be practicing today. | talk |

| Shizuku | I imagine it has some sentimental value to you. | talk |
| Haruka | Yes, I suppose so. | talk |
| Airi | Let's save the trip down memory lane for later. We've<br>got it booked only for a couple hours, so let's start<br>stretching. | talk |

| Minori | Aw, that's such a shame! | talk |

| Airi | Hm, you can stretch your legs pretty far apart. Can you<br>do it with your upper body flat on the floor? | talk |
| Minori | Nope. I can only do it as far as almost touching the floor<br>with my elbows. | talk |
| Shizuku | Isn't that good enough? | talk |
| Minori | What about you girls? | talk |
| Airi | I can easily put my elbows on the floor. Look. | talk |
| Minori | Wow, you really did it! And you, Haruka? | talk |
| Haruka | I can go...this far. | talk |
| Minori | Wow, you can go flat! | talk |
| Haruka | If you're not flexible, you're more prone to injury,<br>apparently. | talk |
| Airi | Can you do a split standing up? | talk |
| Haruka | Like this? | talk |
| Minori | Are you made of rubber?! You could star in a circus! | talk |
| Airi | Or become a ballerina. | talk |
| Shizuku | Hehe. Rhythmic gymnastics is another career option for you. | talk |
| Haruka | Thanks, but I just want to be an idol. | talk |
| Minori | Haruka, what sort of exercises do I need to do to be<br>flexible like you? | talk |
| Haruka | It's enough if you stretch after taking a bath. | talk |
| Haruka | You don't need to do anything hardcore. | talk |
| Minori | But I do stretch after bath! What am I doing wrong? | talk |
| Shizuku | How long do you stretch for, Haruka? | talk |
| Haruka | About an hour at a time. | talk |

| Airi | You've been keeping this up for years. That's commitment. | talk |
| Minori | Yeah! Haruka, you're just so amazing! | talk |
| Minori | I'll do my stretches daily from now on too! | talk |

| Airi | That's not very realistic, don't you think? | talk |
| Minori | But... | talk |
| Haruka | Minori, you mustn't do anything excessive. I understand<br>that you're impatient, but it's better to get into the<br>habit gradually. | talk |
| Minori | You're right! | talk |
| Haruka | Let's continue our stretches. Minori, want me to push<br>your back? | talk |
| Minori | That'll be great, thanks! | talk |

| Haruka | Good morning. | talk |
| An | Welcome... Huh? Haruka? | talk |
| Haruka | Do you have time to talk? | talk |
| An | Sure. We just opened and there aren't any customers, yet. | talk |
| An | What made you come all the way here on a holiday morning? | talk |
| Haruka | I wanted to tell you something... | talk |
| Haruka | I've decided to try to be an idol again. | talk |
| An | That's great! | talk |
| Haruka | That's all, really. I just wanted you to know. | talk |
| An | Hehe. You're so polite, Haruka. | talk |
| An | Since you're already here, you might as well let me know what made you change your mind? Support from fans? | talk |
| Haruka | Something like that.<br>I know the fans are waiting for me... | talk |
| Haruka | But that's not the direct reason. I have a schoolmate who said she's aspiring to become an idol because of me. | talk |
| Haruka | She's the reason. | talk |
| An | Oh?<br>What's she like? | talk |
| Haruka | Honestly, I initially thought she was a bit too plain for an aspiring idol. | talk |
| Haruka | But she works harder than anyone I know and never gives up no matter how many times she's rejected at auditions. | talk |
| Haruka | An idol isn't just a beautiful spark of light. Reality is harsh to us, and a lot of things happen backstage that fans don't know about. | talk |
| Haruka | She understands all of that and still thinks that idols are amazing. | talk |
| Haruka | She insists that idols can really bring hope to people. | talk |
| An | Oh wow... | talk |
| Haruka | She showed me. | talk |
| Haruka | That I did bring hope to someone after all. | talk |
| Haruka | That's why I wanted to start all over again. | talk |
| An | Haruka, your face is so clear now. | talk |
| Haruka | Really? | talk |
| Haruka | But I think you may be right.<br>What used to look pale and gray to me has recently started to glow so brilliantly. | talk |
| An | Hehe, I'm really happy for you. | talk |
| An | Oh, if you ever need a place to perform, let me know, okay? | talk |
| An | I can probably introduce you to most of the event venues around this area... | talk |
| An | And I know this guy who knows a lot of people too. Whatever it is, I'm sure we can be of some help. | talk |
| Haruka | Thank you.<br>Don't mind if I take you up on that offer one day, then. | talk |
| An | Not at all! | talk |
| Haruka | Thanks for talking with me today.<br>I'm glad I came. | talk |
| An | Me too! | talk |
| An | Drop by again whenever you like!<br>Next time, bring the girl you mentioned just now, too. | talk |
| Haruka | Yes, I will.<br>Thank you for everything! | talk |
| An | Sure, see you! | talk |

| Haruka | One, two, three, four...<br>Okay, that should be enough stretching. | talk |
| Haruka | (It's nice to be practicing with Minori and the others again.) | talk |
| Haruka | (I did what I could on my own when I was busy with the committee, but it's always better to train together.) | talk |
| Minori | Hello, Haruka! | talk |
| Shizuku | Now that the Sports Festival's behind us, you can join us for practice again, right? | talk |
| Haruka | Of course. Thank you for letting me skipping our sessions to work on committee stuff. | talk |
| Minori | Oh, it was no problem whatsoever! | talk |
| Airi | Yeah. And good job with the Sports Festival! It turned out great! | talk |
| Shizuku | I enjoyed being able to take part with my classmates too. It was a very memorable event. | talk |
| Haruka | Hearing that makes all my effort worth it!<br>But I have thank you too, Airi, Shizuku... | talk |
| Haruka | I heard from another girl on the committee that it was you two who got the credit-based students to participate. | talk |
| Airi | Oh that? | talk |
| Haruka | A lot of students here are in the same line of work as us, so you hardly ever see them and don't have many event options to choose from even if they do show up. | talk |
| Haruka | We ended up investing a lot of time into figuring out how many would actually show up and how they could enjoy themselves... | talk |
| Haruka | But thanks to you, enough of those girls agreed to come and we were able to have some of them in every competition. Even the upperclassmen were surprised by this. | talk |
| Haruka | And it wasn't just the second years. You even went around to the first and third year students, and got them to sign up too! | talk |
| Minori | Wow! That's amazing, Airi, Shizuku! | talk |
| Shizuku | Oh, it was nothing really. | talk |

| Haruka | Regardless, it really worked. | talk |
| Haruka | And it was the first years who took the initiative by saying they wanted to make it better than ever... | talk |
| Haruka | Which is why it became important to see how we can get the others to go along with us. | talk |
| Airi | I see...<br>But your worries might've been misplaced. | talk |
| Haruka | Huh? | talk |
| Shizuku | A second-year committee member visited our class a few times too, encouraging us to join. | talk |
| Airi | And that third-year girl! Your committee members seemed so invested in it, we thought we should do something to help too. | talk |
| Haruka | Really..?<br>I had no idea. | talk |
| Airi | I bet they were all inspired by you first years! | talk |
| Minori | Wow, what a story!<br>That's incredible! | talk |
| Haruka | Yeah. I'm surprised myself. | talk |
| Haruka | (Come to think of it, Saki said that older students helped her make the list of participants for each event...) | talk |
| Haruka | Well, I'm glad it worked out... | talk |

| Minori | That's great, Haruka! | talk |
| Haruka | Yeah. | talk |

| An | Hey, Haruka...? | talk |
| An | You looked like you had something on your mind just now. Is something wrong? | talk |
| Haruka | No, not really. I was just reminiscing about all the fun I had during the School Festival. | talk |
| An | Oh, okay.<br>If that's the case, you should be smiling more. | talk |
| Haruka | Wouldn't it seem a bit strange if I just started laughing to myself in public, even if I was thinking about some fun memories...? | talk |
| Haruka | Also, I didn't know that a certain friend of mine would call out to me soon afterwards because they were worried. | talk |
| An | What would you do without me?<br>Is what I would've said, but I noticed the look on your face after I called out to you. | talk |
| An | I honestly just happened to see you walking by and decided to ask where you were going. | talk |
| An | I mean, we're best friends, but I can't exactly tell how you're feeling just by seeing your back. | talk |

| An | Alright, Miss Perfect, try telling how I feel just based on my back. | talk |
| Haruka & An | Hehe.<br>Haha. | talk |
| An | Anyway, I had a ton of fun during the Miya Girls School Festival. | talk |
| Haruka | Hehe. Despite getting chased all around campus by a zombie wolf? | talk |

| Haruka | Of course. You thought I didn't? | talk |
| Haruka | Remember how you dove into my class and left a second later while Kohane was chasing you? | talk |
| An | Huh? I did? | talk |
| Haruka | Yeah, you did. But you were so desperate to get away from the zombie wolf that I didn't get a chance to ask you what was going on. | talk |
| An | Hang on. Your class did that animal cafe, right? | talk |
| An | Oh, that explains why it seemed so familiar when I went with Kohane and the others later! | talk |
| Haruka | An... I guess you were genuinely terrified. | talk |
| Haruka | At the same time, a zombie wolf's completely different from a ghost. Why did it scare you so much? | talk |
| An | Why?! I mean, that mask...<br>It looked so real! | talk |
| An | I learned beforehand that Kohane's class would be doing a haunted house, so I tried my best to prepare myself mentally for it... | talk |
| An | But I don't like what I don't like! | talk |
| Haruka | By the way, how did you prepare yourself for it? | talk |
| An | Well... I tried telling myself that things are gonna jump out at me and that they're people, not monsters... | talk |
| Haruka | I see. However... | talk |
| Haruka | Hehe. It didn't work, did it? | talk |
| An | Cut me some slack.<br>I was sure that my plan would work! | talk |
| An | Seriously, I wasn't expecting to run into a zombie wolf right off the bat...! | talk |
| Haruka | Yeah, I guess that makes sense...<br>A surprise like that would throw anyone into a panic. | talk |
| An | Exactly!<br>I never would've seen that coming! | talk |
| Haruka | Well, it is a haunted house, so that's not really an excuse... | talk |
| Haruka | But I'm glad you got to enjoy it in the end. | talk |
| An | Same. My heart raced because of how scary it was, but I guess that's sorta what haunted houses are all about. | talk |
| An | So, I'm glad that I got to enjoy it as an actual haunted house. | talk |
| Haruka | Then, why don't you try the haunted house at PXL the next time you visit? | talk |
| An | What?! | talk |
| Haruka | It's pretty similar to the haunted house that Kohane's class made, so I'm sure you'll be fine, An. | talk |
| An | You're joking! That haunted house isn't as scary as I thought it was going to be? | talk |
| Haruka | It really isn't. You become Phenny as he has a nightmare that's filled with skeletons who keep saying that they're going to “fry you up”. | talk |
| An | Haha! Fry you up?! Like chicken?! | talk |
| Haruka | I'm not kidding. Some even talked about frying you up like “yummy wings” and “juicy tenders”. | talk |
| Haruka | Oh, I heard that there's even one that says, “I'm gonna turn you into bang bang chicken!” If you find it, something good's supposed to happen. | talk |
| An | Really?! I've never heard of anything like that! | talk |
| Haruka | That's probably because you usually cover your ears when people talk about this kind of stuff. | talk |
| An | Oh, yeah. True... | talk |
| Haruka | Hehe. The next time we get to go to PXL with Minori and Kohane, we can give the haunted house a try. | talk |

| Haruka | See? Then, let's all go again sometime. | talk |
| An | Okay! | talk |
